## Common Saddle Features

A secure saddle with bridle for riding long and comfortably.

Variable Before crafting the Saddle, determine whether it is for Medium, Large or Gargantuan Creature.

Saddling Not all creatures want to be saddled. A Traveler must Tame a creature before attempting to saddle it.

## Everflame Pot

Supplies: 1

1 Murrow Rendered Oil and 1 Rauka Thermal Oils

|   DC | Rarity   | CraftingTime   | Tool Proficiency     |
|------|----------|----------------|----------------------|
|    9 | Common   | 1 Day Focused  | Alchemist's Supplies |

A small metal cylinder with a wick, it burns long with a curiously purple flame.

Ever Burning Once lit, the pot stays aflame for 10 days without running out. It cannot be doused by water or wind, but can be closed by covering the top spout. The dark red 6 inch flame lights a 30 ft. Sphere, and can warm the area similar to a campfire, though the cylinder remains cool to the touch.

## Eventide Tent

## Supplies: 3

1 Lexati Furred Patagonia, 4 Skyelarx Phalanges Bones, 1 Nicalliath Scent Gland

|   DC | Rarity   | CraftingTime   | Tool Proficiency      |
|------|----------|----------------|-----------------------|
|   10 | Common   | 1 Day Focused  | Leatherworker's Tools |

While dark and uncomfortably musky, the tent offers a quiet and restful night's sleep. This tent is large enough to house up to 4 Medium creatures, its scent repels most creatures and allows for a comfortably uneventful, if a little musky night.

Predators Musk Creatures are naturally repelled by the scent of the tent, unwilling to approach within 60 ft. without fighting their own basal instincts. CR 1 Beasts and below Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 16, to break through the compulsion and approach. CR 2 Beasts and above Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 12, to muster the will to approach. Apex Predators fear little and do not need to perform this test.

Fit firmly on one's feet, the paddles allow the wearer to reach new speeds beneath the waves. The Golia Paddles can be worn over other footwear.

Natural Swimmer While wearing the Golia Paddles, you gain an additional swim speed of 20 ft. This bonus applies even if you doe not possess a natural swim speed.

Vicious Burst As a Bonus Action, the wearer can perform a Dash Action while underwater.

An aroma of death lingers on the talisman, lending the user a feeling of natural balance. While  wearing  the  Scavenger's  Tryst  talisman,  you  take  1  less  damage  from Necrotic  damage  and  gain  +1  to  your  roll  when  Scavenging  creatures  for Components (pg. 248)

## Tides Breath [2.]

## Supplies: 3

1 Strands Stalk, 4 Icullus Brim Membranes, 2 Daellash Gill Branches

|   DC | Rarity   | CraftingTime   | Tool Proficiency      |
|------|----------|----------------|-----------------------|
|   14 | Uncommon | 1 Day Focused  | Leatherworker's Tools |

While uncomfortable to wear at first, breathing underwater quickly becomes second nature.

Undertide Gills Enables the wearer to breathe underwater as if it was air. Daellash Reserves The wearer can hold their breath for up to 10 minutes before needing to make a Constitution Saving Throw. After the initial 10 minutes, they must make a DC 10 Constitution Saving Throw at the end of each subsequent minute or gain one level of Exhaustion. If the Saving Throw fails, the wearer can't hold their breath, and normal suffocation rules apply.

## Darkling Band

## Supplies: 3

1 Streamweed Strand, 2 Acrynn Hollow Antennae, 4 Lanceleth Muggy Antenna

|   DC | Rarity   | CraftingTime   | Tool Proficiency      |
|------|----------|----------------|-----------------------|
|   15 | Uncommon | 1 Day Focused  | Leatherworker's Tools |

The tremor sensing antennae of two blind beasts, resonates with a kind of vibrating sight. Quiver Step While wearing the Darkling Band, you gain Tremorsense of up to 60 ft., and through a weak form of echolocation, you can perceive the direction and proximity of large objects and walls in total darkness within 30 ft..

Danger  Sense While  wearing  the  Darkling  Band  headband,  you  cannot  be Surprised by concealed creatures within 30 ft. of you.

## Kit Kradle [8.]

## Supplies: 3

1 Basket Cotton Clump, 1 Omaterssum Pouch Hide, 1 Draffer Woolly Hide

|   DC | Rarity   | CraftingTime   | Tool Proficiency      |
|------|----------|----------------|-----------------------|
|   15 | Rare     | 2 Days Focused | Leatherworker's Tools |

Like a mother, the carrier's soft pouch lining provides a warm welcoming embrace.

Creature Carrier You can place a single Small or smaller Creature into the Kit Cradle. Animal Handling (Wisdom) check : DC 16. Success: The creature rests within the Kit Cradle for 1 hour before wriggling out. Failure: The creature resists and the wearer must wait 1 minute before trying again, any additional attempts are made with Disadvantage for 1 hour.

Tiny Tackle As a Bonus Action, you can order the creature resting within it to leave and attack another visible creature with 30 ft.. Dexterity Saving Throw: DC 14, target creature. Failure: The  target  takes  7  (2d6)  Piercing  Damage.  Once  out,  you  must perform the Creature Carrier Action to place the Creature back within the Kit Cradle.

## Trawlbreath Lamp

## Supplies: 3

1 Dulleyed Cattail, 1 Trawlauth Bacterial Pipe and 1 Suffocating Sludge, 1 Gharril Oculoid

|   DC | Rarity   | CraftingTime   | Tool Proficiency     |
|------|----------|----------------|----------------------|
|   15 | Rare     | 2 Days Focused | Alchemist's Supplies |

Billowing bacteria disperses like a cloying cloud, congealing in the breather's lungs. The Trawlbreath Lamp holds enough oil to remain lit for an hour before needing to be refilled with 1 Trawlauth Suffocating Sludge.

Dense Fog While the lamp is lit, it emits a thick undulating blue fog that spreads to  fill  a  30  ft.  Sphere  centered  on  the  lamp.  This  area  is  shrouded  in  magical darkness that Darkvision cannot penetrate.

Billowing Bacteria Constitution Saving Throw: DC 16, any creature that starts its turn within the fog. Failure: Target creature begins to suffocate. The creature has the Incapacitated condition while within the fog.

Dispersing the Fog Any  wind  or  gust  of  air  of  at  least  10  miles  per  hour  can disperse the fog in 1 minute.

## Martial Weapons

<!-- image -->

The sharp barbed bone, perfectly suited to hook nearly anything in the Fenlands. Piercing Throw When a thrown mirelance  hits,  it  remains  embedded  in  the target and may not be used until pulled freed from the target. Until the barbed head is removed, the harpooned creature will take 1 Piercing damage per turn at the beginning of its turn. The barb can be removed by taking an Action to pull or cut out the barbed head, causing 1d4 Piercing damage when removed.

Reel You can use a Bonus Action to retract the harpoon's rope up to 20 ft., pulling the pierced creature closer to you. The target must succeed on a DC 14 Strength Saving Throw or be pulled up to 20 ft. towards you and takes 1 Piercing damage.

Deathly  Pinch Trigger: When  a  creature  steps  upon  the  Grave  Stepper  trap. Response  -  Dexterity  Saving  Throw: DC  15,  the  creature  that  triggered  the  trap. |   10 | Common   | 1/2 Day Focused | Alchemist's Supplies |

Floating amongst other lily pads, the readied mine can trigger with but the gentlest of waves. With a Utilize Action you can place the Lilypin upon a calm water surface, it will activate within 1 minute as the Lilyscales soak.

Lilyburst Trigger: When a creature touches it or disturbs the water around it in within 5 ft. Response - Dexterity Saving Throw: DC 13, each creature in a 20 ft. Sphere centered on the Lilypin trap. Failure: 10 (3d6) Piercing damage and the creature has spikes impaled in their flesh. Impaled creatures take 3 (1d6) Piercing damage at the beginning of their turns until the spikes are a removed, all spikes can be removed  with  a  single  successful  DC  12  Wisdom  (Medicine)  check  or  a  DC  16 Strength check.

Detecting DC  16  Wisdom  (Perception)  check  to  notice  the  Lilypin  trap  if  it  is hidden amongst Fenlands or equivalent aquatic plants. If it is not surrounded by similarly looking plants, or none at all, it has a DC 8 Wisdom (Perception) check to ascertain whether it is a natural plant or not.

Disarming A creature must first approach the Lilypin without disturbing the water by performing a DC 12 Dexterity (Slight of Hand) check to pick it up. Success: The Lilypin trap is disarmed and can be re-used. Failure: The trap explodes as if triggered.

## Pacifist Pins [41.]

Supplies: 1

24 Larkiera Piercing Tooth, 1 Gurubara Cynobacteria Extract

|   DC | Rarity   | CraftingTime    | Tool Proficiency   |
|------|----------|-----------------|--------------------|
|   11 | Common   | 1/4 Day Focused | Weaver's Tools     |

The slight pinprick of pain is quickly replaced by a curiously numbing calmness and peace. With a Utilize action you can spread the Pacifist Pins on the ground to cover a 5 ft. square area.

Peaceful  Pinning Trigger: Any  creature  that  moves  into  the  area. Response  Dexterity Saving Throw: DC 15, each creature within the 5 ft. square space. Failure: The creature immediately stops moving and takes 2 (1d4) Piercing damage as the pins are embedded into the creature. Impaled creatures take 1 Piercing damage at the beginning of their turns, have their walking speed reduced by 10 ft. and has Disadvantage on Initiative and Intimidation rolls until the pins are a removed. All pins can be removed with a single successful DC 10 Wisdom (Medicine) check or a DC 14 Strength check.

## [43.]

## Supplies: 1

16 Scorpithine Pygidium Petal, 1 Daugon Ambergris, 1 Scavick Musk Oil Vial

|   DC | Rarity   | CraftingTime   | Tool Proficiency   |
|------|----------|----------------|--------------------|
|   12 | Common   | 1 Day Focused  | Alchemist Supplies |

Beasts of all sizes flock to the alluring scent, sometimes more than the Traveler bargained for. Luring Scent 12 Uses -You may Utilize the Lure by painting a 1 ft. by 1 ft. square on a dry surface. The liquid then draws the attention of all Beasts within a 300 ft. radius for 30 minutes. Wisdom Saving Throw: DC 14, beasts within a 30 ft. Sphere originated  on  the  painted  square. Failure: Target  creature  has  Disadvantage against Perception checks for the duration of the Lure.

| PyrefootMine [40.]   | Supplies: 1   |
|----------------------|---------------|

1 Baughur Chambered Stomach and 1 Congealed Blubber, 6 Bloomux Bloomspines

|   DC | Rarity   | CraftingTime    | Tool Proficiency     |
|------|----------|-----------------|----------------------|
|   12 | Common   | 1/2 Day Focused | Alchemist's Supplies |

Hunting is not always a stealthy affair, and sometimes explosives are needed. With a Utilize Action you can plant the Pyrefoot Mine into the ground, activating it  by  gently  pushing  the  pressure  plate  until  a  click  is  heard.  Triggering  the Pyrefoot Mine explodes when a creature steps on it or disturbs it.

Pyre Fire Trigger: When creature steps on the mine. Response - Dexterity Saving Throw: DC 15, the creature that triggered the mine. Failure: 14 (4d6) Fire damage and catches fire, causing an additional 3 (1d6) Fire damage at the start of the each of its turns for 1 minute. A creature can use an Action to extinguish the flames on themselves or to help an another, ending the ongoing damage on a successful DC 11 Dexterity check.

Detecting DC 16 Wisdom (Perception) check to notice the buried mine.

Disarming A creature can use an Action to disarm the Mine with a successful DC 16 Dexterity check using Thieves' Tools by separating the pressure plate. Success: The Pyrefoot mine is disarmed and can be re-used. Failure: The mine explodes as if triggered.

## Glassmaw Pit

Supplies: 1

2 Skyelarx Hooked Claw, 8 Mollucite Glassy Shard Teeth, 1 Cerrophol Hollowed Mantle

|   DC | Rarity   | CraftingTime    | Tool Proficiency   |
|------|----------|-----------------|--------------------|
|   12 | Common   | 1/4 Day Focused | Tinker Tools       |

The greatest trappers know how to put what nature has evolved to deadly use. With a Utilization Action, bury the Glassmaw Pit into the ground, lightly covering the ring of teeth with soil.

Glass  Grip Trigger:  When  a  Medium  or  smaller  creature  steps  upon  the Glassmaw  Pit  trap. Response  -  Dexterity  Saving  Throw: DC  16,  the  creature  that triggered  the  trap. Failure: 10  (3d6)  Piercing  damage  and  the  target  has  the Prone  and  Restrained  condition  (escape  DC  12)  as  their  limb  falls  into  the spiked cavity. When extracting their limb from the trap, the backwards facing teeth  cut  into  their  flesh,  causing  10  (3d6)  Slashing  damage  upon  removal. Detecting DC 14 Wisdom (Perception) check to notice the Glassmaw Pit trap if it is hidden under the dirt by some glassy teeth shining through.

Disarming A creature must first approach the Glassmaw Pit without disturbing it  and  performing  a  DC  14  Dexterity  check  to  dig  and  pick  it  up. Success: The Glassmaw Pit trap is disarmed and can be re-used. Failure: The trap closes tightly and cannot be used again.

## Paralytic Mucilage

Supplies: 1

1 Mollucite Viscid Fluid, 1 Glomunder Paralytic Tendril, 1 Scorpithine Scorpithi Toxin

|   DC | Rarity   | CraftingTime                  | Tool Proficiency     |
|------|----------|-------------------------------|----------------------|
|   13 | Common   | 1/2 Day Focused 1 Day Patient | Alchemist's Supplies |

Apply carefully, too many Travelers have been found lying unresponsive next to their own trap. 6 Uses -Applying this mucilage to any surface with a Utilization Action and lasts for 1 day, it dries invisibly within 1 minute and covers a 1 ft. by 1 ft. square.

Numbing Salve Trigger: When a creature comes into contact with the mucilage. Response - Constitution Saving Throw: DC 12, the creature in contact. Failure: Target has the Paralyzed condition and repeats the save at the end of each of its turns, ending the effect on itself on a success. After 3 minutes, it succeeds automatically.

## Weald Watcher

Supplies: 1

1 Jhotus Optic Parasite, 1 Korrask Feeler Tendril, 1 Scorpithine Stiletto Leg

|   DC | Rarity   | CraftingTime   | Tool Proficiency   |
|------|----------|----------------|--------------------|
|   16 | Rare     | 1 Day Focused  | Tinker Tools       |

Parasites only ever look for a creature to settle upon, and the Jhotus optic parasite's natural reaction, paired with a thin bladed limb makes for a surprisingly effective reactive guard. With a Utilization Action, place the Weald Ward on the ground, it will activate when the leg is pressed down and clicks. By spending 10 minutes near the Weald Ward trap, the parasite  becomes  accustom  to  your  presence  and  won't  trigger towards you.

Stalking Eye Trigger: When a creature approaches within a 30 ft. Sphere centered on the trap. Response - Constitution Saving Throw: DC 17, the creature that triggered the  trap. Failure: 4  (1d8)  Poison  damage  and  has  the  Poisoned  condition.  The creature  can  repeat  the  save  at  the  end  each  of  its  turns,  ending  the  effect  on itself on a success. While Poisoned, the creature slowly becomes paralyzed, after 1 minute the poison sets in the target creature has the Paralyzed condition for 1 hour. Detecting DC 14 Wisdom (Perception) check to notice the Weald Ward trap to see its questing parasite eye.

Disarming A creature must first approach the Weald Ward slowly with a DC 17 Dexterity (Stealth) check to pick it up. Success: The Weald Ward trap is disarmed and can be re-used. Failure: The trap reacts as if triggered.

## Tainted Mulch

Supplies: 3

1 Vial of Vigael, 1 Trikautrae Concentrated Digestion Enzyme, 1 Bloomux Curled Proboscis, 1 Lexati Jade Eye, 1 Jhotus Malodorous Mucus

|   DC | Rarity   | CraftingTime   | Tool Proficiency      |
|------|----------|----------------|-----------------------|
|   16 | Rare     | 1 Day Focused  | Leatherworker's Tools |

Mixing the worst of the digestive juices with the jade eye of a Lexati produces an aggressive growth serum that makes any plant or fungus dangerously poisonous.

Virulent Growth With a Utilize Action, you can spread the fertilizer liquid across a 5 ft. by 5 ft. square on any given plant or fungal growth. The clear liquid then turns the flora poisonous to the touch for 7 days, without harming the flora as it ingests the toxins.

Toxic Touch Trigger: When a creature moves through or comes in contact with the flora within the Tainted Mulch's poisoned space. Response - Constitution Saving Throw: DC 17, the creature that triggered the trap. Failure: 18 (4d8) Poison damage and has the Poisoned condition. The creature can repeat the save at the end each of its turns, ending the effect on itself on a success. After 10 minutes, it succeeds automatically.  While  poisoned,  the  target  suffers  a  hacking  cough  and  has Disadvantage on Dexterity (Stealth) checks and suffers one level of Exhaustion.

## Recipe Examples

Whether in the safety of a cozy camp, or from under a leafy shelter, keeping a weak flame from guttering out in the rain, a well cooked meal can warm the spirit and help prepare for the trials of the day. Travelers who venture out into the wilds can safely live off the land when they know what to look for and how to cook it. Below can be found eight example recipes, tried and true meals to bolster any Traveler. But this list is meager compared to what is possible out there with so many potential ingredients, and it is recommended that a Traveler try their own hand at preparing meals with the flora and fauna that inhabit Sol'Kesh, noting down their findings and exploring what the island has to offer.

## Plainsflame Ribs

Lay tartakka ribs over a simmering fire and brush them with sweet and spicy ilyrana sauce that caramelizes into a glossy glaze. As that cooks, knead crushed bloomstone grains with a little water until  a  coarse  dough  forms,  heat  on  a  flat  stone  near  the  ribs. Within moments, you will have a hearty meal that invigorates, strengthens your limbs and lends a certain fiery confidence. Benefits Gain Advantage on Athletic (Strength) and Intimidation (Charisma) rolls and gain +2d4 Temporary Hit Points for 1 hour.

## Stubborn Stew

Mash  the  mustermold  mushrooms  with  a  healthy  amount  of water into a thick creamy sauce, add sliced chunks of sacrissian marbled  tail  meat.  When  thoroughly  cooked,  add  crushed thornet garnet for a tasty sweetness to center the mind.

Benefits Gain  Resistance  to  Psychic  damage,  Saving  Throw Advantage against the Charmed condition and +1d10 Temporary Hit Points for 1 hour.

## Nights Eye Pie

Sculpt the waxcap mushroom into a bowl-like shape and bake for an hour, when firm, fill it with slices of taddil fish, sea bead fruit slices and water. Continue to bake for another hour and serve for a  wholesome meal that alights the night sky with a clarity and hastens the limbs with vigor.

Benefits Gain Darkvision for up to 30 ft., +5 Movement Speed and Advantage on Nature (Wisdom) checks for Tracking for 1 hour.

## Many Moon Salad

Cut the long streamweed leaves and gently wash in cold water. S lice  the  glowing  eloom  into  quarters  and  mix  into  the  leaves. Crush  the  unsundew  bulbs  into  a  creamy  glowing  sauce  and drizzle them over the salad and serve, preferably at night to enjoy the iridescent ingredients.

Benefits Gain Advantage on Saving Throws against the Frightened condition, +5 Movement Speed and +1d6 Temporary Hit Points for 1 hour.

## Long Stride Drumstick

Grill the large hectatrex drumstick over a low fire until it becomes a rich brown, then cut the ferastack column into thick slices and place them on the grill when the meat is ready to be flipped. After waiting a few moments to allow the ferastack slices to crisp, pull both from the grill and enjoy an invigorating meal that fills one with a near exhaustible stamina.

Benefits Gain +10 Movement Speed and do not count the first level of Exhaustion suffered for 1 hour.

## Saltspray Hair

Boil a pot of water and add brinebloom stalks to soak and soften. While  it  boils,  slice  cerropohol  and  miremere  meat  into  small cubes and sear in a hot pan. Once the stalks are soft, stir in the meat cubes and let it continue boiling for a few moments before straining the water and serving the warm fortifying meal.

Benefits Gain  +1  Constitution,  +1d4  and  +1d6  Temporary  Hit Points for 1 hour.

## Stalkers Steaks

Cut the skith tail segment and grinfin meat into thin slices and sear over a hot pan. When cooked, stack the slices with a drizzle of  sanguinite  sap  between  the  slices  and  sprinkle  heavily  with coral salt. The salty bites quicken the blood and quiet the nerves. Benefits Gain  +1 Dexterity and  +1 to your Initiative rolls for  2  hours,  Advantage  on  Stealth  (Dexterity)  checks,  and +2d4 Temporary Hit Points for 1 hour.

## Rose Petal Shake

Chop the solmundis tongue into tiny pieces and crush them into a fine paste, then blend the meat into a pot of pebbleback auhk milk  and  boil  for  10  minutes.  When  thoroughly  steaming,  add in sliced scorpithine pygidium petals and shake. Once the warm pink  milkshake  is  drunk,  the  drinker  finds  themselves  able  to sway others with an ease like never before.

Benefits Gain  Advantage  on  Persuasion  (Charisma),  Insight (Wisdom)  and  Animal  Handling  (Wisdom)  checks  for  1  hour.

<!-- image -->

Pinscale

Length - 6 in.

This river fish swims awkwardly by  twisting  its  head  back  and  forth to  deter  predators  with  its  wickedly sharp  facial  spikes.  While  primarily used for defense, the protruding spines easily get caught on fresh water grass, allowing the fish to nibble on the move

## Journey's End

A  wet  rope  slides  across  calloused  fingers  as  the  Traveler  pulls  in  the  day's  catch.  Salty  water  streams  off  the  hooked  fish  while  it  tugs upwards,  rising  up  the  sheer  granite  cliff  to  the  Traveler's  simple  wooden  hut.  Its  blue  scales  glinting  in  the  high  noon  sun,  a  small  crab desperately  clinging  onto  the  fish's  red  finned  tail.  "Well  then,  looks  like  we  have  a  little  traveler  of  our  own."  Plucking  the  crab  from their  catch  to  throw  it  off  the  cliff  into  the  crashing  waves  below,  the  Traveler  felt  fairly  confident  that  its  hard  shell  will  allow  the  critter  to survive  the  fall.  Letting  out  a  relaxed  sigh,  they  then  pull  out  a  thin  descaling  knife  from  their  Maker's  Kit  and  prepares  the  fish  for  lunch.

Humming a song from a distant land, the quiet peace is broken by an erupting cacophony of gulls. Clutching the knife in alarm the Traveler quickly stands up and steps over to the cliff to view the distant rocky outcroppings below, expecting the worst this island can offer. Dozens of black beaked fingulls shriek and fly haphazardly away from the splashing commotion as black webbed wings beat furiously at the water surface, thrashing violently.

Yelling in alarm the Traveler runs back to their hut. Quickly rummaging near the door they grip the bone and coral bow, pulling the gut string from their belt pouch, rushing to help.  Within moments the Traveler stands alert at the cliff edge, an arrow knocked and trained on the winged beast, waiting for the right time to let loose. With a triumphant cry, the skyelarx pulls itself out of the water, its wide wings beating powerfully, struggling to lift itself and the dead weight of its prey. Lowering their arrow and smirking, the Traveler watches their winged friend awkwardly fly back home with a long daellash hanging limp in its clawed talons.

With a wet crash the massive eel drops onto the flagstones near the fire and the skyelarx nimbly lands next to it, its golden eyes clearly pleased with itself as it compares the Traveler's meager half-descaled catch to its own.  "Alright, alright, you win. Let me get my knife and we can both eat well tonight."
